● The plan is written. Summary of what it covers and what I found while
  researching it.

  The real gap in the incumbent. I read rtionline.gov.in's own guidelines and
  FAQ. Registration is explicitly optional and buys nothing: cases are tracked
  by a registration number plus a typed email, there is no "my filings" page
  anywhere, and records are purged after three years. The portal covers Central
  authorities only and says so, but never asks what your problem is and tells
  you which government owns it. You get one 3000 character textarea with no
  drafting help. After submission it goes silent, so nothing counts your thirty
  days under section 7(1) or warns you the day the section 19(1) appeal window
  opens. The first appeal makes you retype everything.

  Numbers that back it, all needing re-confirmation before they go on screen:
  2,80,353 applications transferred between authorities in 2024-25, up roughly
  18 percent (CIC Annual Report). Over four lakh appeals pending across 29
  commissions at 30 June 2025, Telangana needing about 29 years at its present
  rate, and no penalty imposed in roughly 98 percent of eligible cases (Satark
  Nagrik Sangathan). Plus the 2,797 of 3,128 already parsed and sourced in
  evidence.ts (../../WEB-DEV-PROJECTS/adhikaar/src/lib/data/evidence.ts).

  What survives untouched. The deterministic core is genuinely good and the plan
  wraps it rather than rewriting it: the Seventh Schedule classifier, the
  eighteen rule linter, the deadline arithmetic, the pdf-lib generator, and the
  citation render gate that throws rather than showing an unsourced legal claim.

  The three decisions worth flagging:

  1. Session cookies are minted with our own HMAC rather than
  createSessionCookie(), because the service account deliberately holds only
  roles/datastore.user and widening it to make login work would undo a
  documented security decision.
  2. The Rights Navigator uses a hand-transcribed corpus with a lexical scorer
  and a hard retrieval floor, not embeddings. Below the floor it says it has no
  source and declines. That decline is a tested path and it is what the video
  should show deliberately.
  3. Scheme eligibility is decided by a pure evaluator, never a model, and each
  scheme carries the verified / conflicting_sources / unverified flag that
  state-fees.ts already uses. Third-party sources disagreed on income thresholds
  during planning, which is exactly why.

  Eleven work packages, each ending green on npm run verify. Research notes,
  build log and submission artifacts go in build-what-india-moves; code stays on
  a branch in the Adhikaar repo.

     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ Context: what exists, and why it is not finished                        │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Adhikaar already exists and it works. It is a Next.js 16 app with 109   │
     │ passing Vitest tests                                                    │
     │ and a deterministic core that does four things no other RTI tool does:  │
     │                                                                         │
     │ 1. It reads a citizen's problem and decides, in plain code, whether the │
     │ subject matter is a                                                     │
     │ Union subject or a State subject under the Seventh Schedule of the      │
     │ Constitution                                                            │
     │ (src/lib/data/seventh-schedule.ts, src/lib/jurisdiction.ts).            │
     │ 2. It runs eighteen legality rules over each drafted question and tells │
     │ you which ones a                                                        │
     │ Public Information Officer can lawfully refuse, citing the section      │
     │ (src/lib/linter/rules.ts).                                              │
     │ 3. It runs the statutory clock: thirty days under section 7(1), forty   │
     │ eight hours on the                                                      │
     │ life or liberty track, thirty days to appeal a deemed refusal under     │
     │ section 19(1), ninety                                                   │
     │ days for a second appeal under section 19(3) (src/lib/deadlines.ts,     │
     │ src/lib/sweep.ts).                                                      │
     │ 4. It builds the actual filed document with pdf-lib, addressed          │
     │ correctly, with the fee                                                 │
     │ declaration and the numbered questions (src/lib/pdf/generate.ts).       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ That core is good. The problem is everything wrapped around it.         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The app was built for an operator. The word "operator" appears          │
     │ throughout the interface.                                               │
     │ The main workspace lives at /docket/[id]. There is a field called       │
     │ "Operator notes". The                                                   │
     │ README describes it as a tool "for the people who file on behalf of     │
     │ others", which is a                                                     │
     │ Common Service Centre volunteer, not a citizen. There is no login.      │
     │ There are no accounts.                                                  │
     │ Anyone who opens the URL sees every case anyone else created.           │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Build What Moves India judges this as a citizen. The rules from the     │
     │ organiser's briefing                                                    │
     │ video, in their own words: rebuild the platform end to end with mock    │
     │ data and mock accounts,                                                 │
     │ provide working test credentials so the team can log in and try it      │
     │ exactly like an end user                                                │
     │ would, only the consumer side is evaluated, it has to open in a         │
     │ browser, and put the energy                                             │
     │ into interfaces and interactions rather than the plumbing. They also    │
     │ said, twice, that the                                                   │
     │ citizen they have in mind is frustrated and short on time and wants     │
     │ something faster and                                                    │
     │ simpler than the site they use today.                                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ So the job is not to add features to an operator tool. The job is to    │
     │ turn a proven engine                                                    │
     │ into something a frustrated citizen can use in eight minutes, sign      │
     │ into, come back to, and                                                 │
     │ be reminded by.                                                         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ The service being rebuilt, and what is actually wrong with it           │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The service is rtionline.gov.in, the Department of Personnel and        │
     │ Training's RTI Online                                                   │
     │ portal, version 2.0. Here is what a citizen actually meets there,       │
     │ verified against the                                                    │
     │ portal's own guidelines and FAQ pages on 29 August 2026.                │
     │                                                                         │
     │ You do not get an account. The FAQ says it directly: "No. You can       │
     │ directly file your RTI                                                  │
     │ on 'Submit Request' tab." Registration is optional and buys you almost  │
     │ nothing. Your case is                                                   │
     │ tracked by a registration number plus the email address you typed. Lose │
     │ the number, lose the                                                    │
     │ case. There is no page anywhere that shows you the applications you     │
     │ have filed. The portal                                                  │
     │ retains RTI cases for three years and then they are gone.               │
     │                                                                         │
     │ You have to already know which government owns your problem. The portal │
     │ covers Central                                                          │
     │ ministries and departments only. It says, in its own words, that you    │
     │ should not use it for                                                   │
     │ state government authorities. It does not ask what your problem is and  │
     │ then tell you. It                                                       │
     │ gives you a cascading Ministry to Department to Public Authority        │
     │ dropdown and expects you to                                             │
     │ pick. If you pick wrong and the correct authority is also on the        │
     │ portal, a Nodal Officer                                                 │
     │ transfers it. If the correct authority is a State authority, DoPT       │
     │ Office Memorandum                                                       │
     │ No. 10/2/2008-IR says a Central Public Information Officer does not     │
     │ have to transfer it at                                                  │
     │ all. It goes back to you. The ten rupees does not.                      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ You get one 3000 character textarea and no help. No warning that a      │
     │ question starting with                                                  │
     │ "why" is asking for an opinion rather than a record, and can be refused │
     │ under section 2(f)                                                      │
     │ without the officer ever reaching a section 8 exemption. No warning     │
     │ that "all files since                                                   │
     │ inception" invites a disproportionate diversion refusal. No warning     │
     │ that asking for a named                                                 │
     │ person's salary now falls under the amended section 8(1)(j), which      │
     │ since 13 November 2025 no                                               │
     │ longer carries a public interest override.                              │
     │                                                                         │
     │ After you submit, the portal goes silent. You get a registration        │
     │ number. Nothing counts                                                  │
     │ the thirty days for you. Nothing tells you the day it lapses that you   │
     │ now have a deemed                                                       │
     │ refusal under section 7(2) and a thirty day window to file a first      │
     │ appeal under section                                                    │
     │ 19(1), free of cost under section 7(6). Miss that window and the remedy │
     │ is gone. The portal                                                     │
     │ does not consider this its job.                                         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The first appeal makes you start over. Select "Submit First Appeal",    │
     │ type the                                                                │
     │ registration number and the email again, fill the form again.           │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Now the scale of what that costs, from official and independent         │

     │ What we are building                                                    │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Adhikaar becomes a citizen-facing civic filing service with an account, │
     │ built around one                                                        │
     │ claim:                                                                  │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ▎ A citizen's problem is one journey. Am I even entitled to something.  │
     │ ▎ What are my rights                                                    │
     │ ▎ here. Which office can lawfully answer me. What do I actually send    │
     │ ▎ them. Every tool that                                                 │
     │ ▎ exists answers exactly one of those and hands you back to yourself    │
     │ ▎ for the other three.                                                  │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Four features, which come straight from the locked recommendation in    │
     │ nsut-innovators-court/hacker-villa/HACKVILLA-NAINITAL-2026-RESEARCH.md  │
     │ section 5.1. Read                                                       │
     │ that section before starting. It contains the scope honesty that must   │
     │ survive into this                                                       │
     │ build.                                                                  │
