     │ │                  │ A guided interview that produces │ New. Sits on    │
     │ the │                                                                   │
     │ │ Conversational   │  a correctly addressed, legally  │  existing       │
     │  │                                                                      │
     │ │ Form Filler      │ clean application                │ pdf-lib         │
     │  │                                                                      │
     │ │                  │                                  │ pipeline.       │
     │  │                                                                      │
     │ ├──────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────┼──────────────── │
     │ ──┤                                                                     │
     │ │                  │ Ask what you can do about a      │ New. The        │
     │ largest │                                                               │
     │ │ Rights Navigator │ tenant, consumer or workplace    │  piece of new   │
     │  │                                                                      │
     │ │                  │ dispute, get an answer that      │ work.           │
     │  │                                                                      │
     │ │                  │ cites the actual section         │                 │
     │  │                                                                      │
     │ ├──────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────┼──────────────── │
     │ ──┤                                                                     │
     │ │ Scheme           │ Answer "do I qualify" for a      │ New. Bounded on │
     │  │                                                                      │
     │ │ Eligibility      │ small set of hand verified       │ purpose.        │
     │  │                                                                      │
     │ │ Reader           │ schemes, with the rule shown     │                 │
     │  │                                                                      │
     │ └──────────────────┴──────────────────────────────────┴──────────────── │
     │ ──┘                                                                     │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Plus the two things the competition brief demands and the current app   │
     │ does not have: real                                                     │
     │ accounts, and a citizen-first interface in Hindi and English with voice │
     │ input.                                                                  │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The handoffs between the four are the product. Not the four features.   │
     │ Anyone can build                                                        │
     │ four pages. The moment that wins the two minute video is one continuous │
     │ flow where a "you do                                                    │
     │ not qualify" answer turns into "then ask them why, in writing", which   │
     │ turns into a                                                            │
     │ jurisdiction-checked RTI draft, which starts a thirty day clock, which  │
     │ drafts the appeal                                                       │
     │ itself when the department goes quiet. Build the handoffs as first      │
     │ class buttons, not as                                                   │
     │ navigation.                                                             │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ Decisions already made, so you do not have to make them                 │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Repository. Extend this repo in place, on a branch. Keep the git        │
     │ history. It is the                                                      │
     │ proof that the deterministic core was engineered rather than generated, │
     │ and a technical judge                                                   │
     │ who opens the repo will see fourteen commits of real work rather than   │
     │ one squashed dump.                                                      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Auth. Firebase Auth. firebase and firebase-admin are already in         │
     │ package.json and                                                        │
     │ Firestore is already the production case store, so this is the cheapest │
     │ correct answer.                                                         │
     │ Email and password, plus Google sign in, plus a prominent one click     │
     │ demo login so a judge                                                   │
     │ never has to type anything.                                             │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Session mechanism, and the trap in it. Do not use                       │
     │ admin.auth().createSessionCookie().                                     │
     │ The service account behind this project holds only                      │
     │ roles/datastore.user, deliberately, and                                 │
     │ minting a Firebase session cookie needs iam.serviceAccounts.signBlob on │
     │ top of that.                                                            │
     │ Widening that service account's permissions to make a login flow work   │
     │ would undo a security                                                   │
     │ decision that is documented in the README as a feature. Instead:        │
     │                                                                         │
     │ 1. Client signs in with the Firebase web SDK and gets an ID token.      │
     │ 2. POST /api/auth/session verifies it with                              │
     │ admin.auth().verifyIdToken(), which the                                 │
     │ existing permissions allow.                                             │
     │ 3. That route mints our own cookie: a JSON payload of { uid, email,     │
     │ name, exp }, HMAC                                                       │
     │ signed with SESSION_SECRET using node crypto.createHmac, set httpOnly,  │
     │ secure,                                                                 │
     │ sameSite: "lax", fourteen day expiry.                                   │
     │ 4. src/lib/auth/session.ts exports getSession() for Server Components   │
     │ and Server Actions,                                                     │
     │ and requireSession() which redirects to /login.                         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Add SESSION_SECRET to .env.example and to the Vercel project            │
     │ environment variables.                                                  │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Language. Full Hindi and English interface, plus voice input. The       │
     │ Devanagari font is                                                      │
     │ already loaded in src/app/layout.tsx as Tiro_Devanagari_Hindi.          │
     │ VoiceDictationButton.tsx                                                │
     │ already exists. Neither needs inventing.                                │
     │                                                                         │
     │ What stays deterministic. Everything that decides. Jurisdiction,        │
     │ legality, deadlines,                                                    │
     │ fees, scheme eligibility, and whether a citation may render. The README │
     │ has a table titled                                                      │
     │ "What this system decides, what it merely proposes" and a one line      │
     │ rule: a model may propose                                               │
     │ and phrase, it may never adjudicate or compute. Every new feature in    │
     │ this build obeys that                                                   │
     │ rule, and the Rights Navigator obeys it in the hardest place, which is  │
     │ why it is the                                                           │
     │ interesting one.                                                        │
     │                                                                         │
     │ What is out of scope, and say so in the interface. No submission to any │
     │ government                                                              │
     │ portal. There is no public API for it and pretending otherwise would be │
     │ dishonest. Adhikaar                                                     │
     │ prepares the document and tells you exactly where to paste or post it.  │
     │ The Rights Navigator                                                    │
     │ is not legal advice and says so on every answer. No claim of            │
     │ comprehensive statutory or                                              │
     │ scheme coverage anywhere.                                               │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ Work packages                                                           │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Follow these in order. Each one ends in a state where npm run verify    │
     │ passes and the app                                                      │
     │ still works. Commit at the end of each. Do not start the next one with  │
     │ the previous one                                                        │
     │ broken.                                                                 │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ WP1: Ownership in the data model                                        │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Right now every case is visible to everyone. Fix that first, because    │
     │ every later package                                                     │
     │ depends on knowing who owns a record.                                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Files: src/lib/types.ts, src/lib/store/types.ts, src/lib/store.ts,      │
     │ src/lib/store/fileStore.ts, src/lib/store/firestoreStore.ts,            │
     │ scripts/seed-cases.ts                                                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ 1. Add ownerUid: string to CaseRecord in src/lib/types.ts, right under  │
     │ status.                                                                 │
     │ 2. Change the CaseStore interface in src/lib/store/types.ts:            │
     │   - listCases(ownerUid: string): Promise<CaseRecord[]>                  │
     │   - getCase(id: string, ownerUid: string): Promise<CaseRecord |         │
     │ undefined>                                                              │
     │   - updateCase(id, ownerUid, patch)                                     │
     │   - deleteCase(id, ownerUid)                                            │
     │ Ownership is checked inside the store, not by the caller. A caller that │
     │ forgets to check                                                        │
     │ is a security bug waiting to happen, and there will be a lot of         │
     │ callers.                                                                │
     │ 3. In firestoreStore.ts, listCases becomes                              │
     │ db.collection("cases").where("ownerUid", "==", ownerUid).get(), then    │
     │ sort by updatedAt                                                       │
     │ descending in JavaScript. Do not chain .orderBy("updatedAt") onto the   │
     │ where.                                                                  │
     │ Firestore requires a composite index for that combination and the       │
     │ deploy will fail at                                                     │
     │ runtime with an index creation link, which is exactly the kind of thing │
     │ that breaks a demo                                                      │
     │ thirty seconds before a judge opens it. Case counts per user are small. │
     │ Sorting in memory                                                       │
     │ is correct here.                                                        │
     │ 4. getCase, updateCase and deleteCase read the document, compare        │
     │ data.ownerUid to the                                                    │
     │ passed uid, and return undefined or false on mismatch. Not an error. A  │
     │ wrong owner                                                             │
     │ should be indistinguishable from a case that does not exist.            │
     │ 5. Mirror all of it in fileStore.ts.                                    │
     │ 6. Update src/lib/actions.ts to thread the uid through from the         │
     │ session. That comes in WP2,                                             │
     │ so for now have it read a constant and mark it with a TODO(WP2)         │
     │ comment.                                                                │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Test: add src/lib/store/ownership.test.ts against fileStore in a temp   │
     │ directory. A                                                            │
     │ case created by uid A is not returned to uid B by getCase, is not in    │
     │ B's listCases, is not                                                   │
     │ mutated by B's updateCase, and is not removed by B's deleteCase. Four   │
     │ assertions minimum.                                                     │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Check: npm run test passes with the new tests. npm run build clean.     │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ WP2: Accounts, sessions, and the demo door                              │
     │                                                                         │
     │ New files:                                                              │
     │ src/lib/auth/firebaseClient.ts     Firebase web SDK singleton           │
     │ src/lib/auth/session.ts            sign, verify, getSession,            │
     │ requireSession                                                          │
     │ src/app/api/auth/session/route.ts  POST to create, DELETE to clear      │
     │ src/app/login/page.tsx             the login screen                     │
     │ src/components/auth/LoginForm.tsx  client component, all three sign-in  │
     │ paths                                                                   │
     │ src/components/auth/SignOutButton.tsx                                   │
     │ middleware.ts                      route guard at the project root      │
     │ Edited: src/components/SiteHeader.tsx, src/lib/actions.ts, .env.example │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The Firebase web SDK needs the public client config, which is a         │
     │ different set of values from                                            │
     │ the admin service account already in the environment. Add to            │
     │ .env.example and to Vercel:                                             │
     │                                                                         │
     │ NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_API_KEY=                                           │
     │ NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_AUTH_DOMAIN=                                       │
     │ NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_PROJECT_ID=                                        │
     │ NEXT_PUBLIC_FIREBASE_APP_ID=                                            │
     │ SESSION_SECRET=                                                         │
     │ DEMO_ACCOUNT_EMAIL=demo@adhikaar.in                                     │
     │ DEMO_ACCOUNT_PASSWORD=                                                  │
     │                                                                         │
     │ These are public by design. Firebase client keys are not secrets. The   │
     │ security boundary is                                                    │
     │ Firestore rules and the admin SDK, not the API key.                     │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The login screen matters more than it sounds. A judge with two hundred  │
     │ submissions open                                                        │
     │ in tabs will bounce off a login wall. So:                               │
     │                                                                         │
     │ - A single large primary button, "Enter as a demo citizen", top of the  │
     │ page, above the form.                                                   │
     │ One click signs into the seeded demo account. No typing.                │
     │ - Directly under it, in a bordered panel, the credentials printed as    │
     │ plain selectable text:                                                  │
     │ demo@adhikaar.in and the password. Some judges will want to type them   │
     │ to prove it is real.                                                    │
     │ Let them.                                                               │
     │ - Then Google sign in, then email and password, then a link to create   │
     │ an account.                                                             │
     │ - One line explaining what happens to their data, because this app      │
     │ holds grievance text that                                               │
     │ can contain personal information.                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ middleware.ts guards /my and /my/* only. The landing page, /rights,     │
     │ /schemes and                                                            │
     │ /methodology stay open, because a judge should be able to see the       │
     │ Rights Navigator work                                                   │
     │ before deciding to sign in.                                             │
     │                                                                         │
     │ After sign in, redirect to /my. Every Server Action in                  │
     │ src/lib/actions.ts calls                                                │
     │ requireSession() first and passes the uid into the store. Remove the    │
     │ TODO(WP2) constant.                                                     │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Check: sign in with the demo button, land on /my, see the seeded cases. │
     │ Sign out. Sign                                                          │
     │ in with a fresh Google account, land on /my, see zero cases and an      │
     │ empty state. Open a case                                                │
     │ id belonging to the demo account while signed in as the fresh account   │
     │ and confirm a 404 rather                                                │
     │ than an error page.                                                     │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ WP3: The citizen shell                                                  │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The word "docket" is a court clerk's word. "Operator" is a call centre  │
     │ word. Both are wrong                                                    │
     │ for someone trying to find out why their pension has not arrived.       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Route moves:                                                            │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ┌──────────────┬──────────┬────────────────────┐                        │
     │ │     From     │    To    │        Why         │                        │
     │ ├──────────────┼──────────┼────────────────────┤                        │
     │ │ /docket      │ /my      │ "My filings"       │                        │
     │ ├──────────────┼──────────┼────────────────────┤                        │
     │ │ /docket/[id] │ /my/[id] │ one filing         │                        │
     │ ├──────────────┼──────────┼────────────────────┤                        │
     │ │ /docket/new  │ /start   │ the interview, WP4 │                        │
     │ └──────────────┴──────────┴────────────────────┘                        │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Keep /docket and /docket/[id] as permanent redirects so any link        │
     │ already shared still                                                    │
     │ works.                                                                  │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Language changes throughout the interface. This is not cosmetic, it is  │
     │ the difference                                                          │
     │ between a tool for a volunteer and a tool for a citizen.                │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ┌──────────────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────── │
     │ ───┐                                                                    │
     │ │               Old                │                 New                │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ ├──────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────── │
     │ ───┤                                                                    │
     │ │ Operator notes                   │ My notes                           │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ ├──────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────── │
     │ ───┤                                                                    │
     │ │ Case                             │ Filing, or "your application"      │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ ├──────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────── │
     │ ───┤                                                                    │
     │ │ Is this the right instrument?    │ Will an RTI actually get you what  │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ │                                  │ you need?                          │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ ├──────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────── │
     │ ───┤                                                                    │
     │ │ Which government, and which      │ Who is legally required to answer  │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ │ office?                          │ you                                │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ ├──────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────── │
     │ ───┤                                                                    │
     │ │ Statutory clock                  │ Your deadline                      │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ ├──────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────── │
     │ ───┤                                                                    │
     │ │ Mark filed and start the clock   │ I have sent it. Start my thirty    │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ │                                  │ days.                              │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ ├──────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────── │
     │ ───┤                                                                    │
     │ │ Run the daily sweep manually     │ Show me what happens when they     │
     │ miss  │                                                                 │
     │ │ (demo control)                   │ the deadline                       │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ ├──────────────────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────── │
     │ ───┤                                                                    │
     │ │ Export                           │ Download your application          │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ └──────────────────────────────────┴─────────────────────────────────── │
     │ ───┘                                                                    │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The /my/[id] page keeps every capability it has today. It changes       │
     │ shape. Today it is nine                                                 │
     │ stacked sections of equal weight, which is a workbench. Make it:        │
     │                                                                         │
     │ - A header stating, in one sentence, where this filing stands right now │
     │ and what the citizen                                                    │
     │ needs to do next. If there is nothing to do, say that.                  │
     │ - A live deadline card, prominent, showing days remaining as a large    │
     │ numeral, the section it                                                 │
     │ comes from, and what happens when it hits zero. When it lapses it turns │
     │ and shows the                                                           │
     │ drafted first appeal, ready.                                            │
     │ - The application itself, questions numbered, each carrying its linter  │
     │ findings inline.                                                        │
     │ - Everything else, which is the fee, the authority reasoning, the notes │
     │ and the plain                                                           │
     │ language copy, in a secondary column or behind disclosure.              │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Keep the design system exactly as it is. The parchment, oxblood and     │
     │ gilt palette in                                                         │
     │ src/app/globals.css, the Fraunces and Source Serif and IBM Plex Mono    │
     │ stack, the square                                                       │
     │ corners, the wax seal. It is genuinely distinctive and it will not look │
     │ like the other 4,999                                                    │
     │ submissions. Do not replace it with a blue and purple SaaS theme.       │
     │ Extend the tokens if you                                                │
     │ need to, replace nothing.                                               │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Check: every route renders. Old /docket URLs redirect. No string        │
     │ containing "operator"                                                   │
     │ or "docket" remains in any user-visible copy. Grep for it.              │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ WP4: The Conversational Form Filler                                     │
     │                                                                         │
     │ This replaces the current intake, which is one page of form fields,     │
     │ with an interview. It is                                                │
     │ the feature that makes the difference between "I have to understand RTI │
     │ to use this" and "I                                                     │
     │ have to describe my problem".                                           │
     │                                                                         │
     │ New files:                                                              │
     │ src/lib/interview/graph.ts        the question graph, pure data         │
     │ src/lib/interview/engine.ts       next question given answers so far,   │
     │ pure function                                                           │
     │ src/lib/interview/engine.test.ts                                        │
     │ src/app/start/page.tsx                                                  │
     │ src/components/interview/Interview.tsx                                  │
     │ src/components/interview/InterviewStep.tsx                              │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Build the interview as a deterministic graph, not an LLM agent. Every   │
     │ step is a node                                                          │
     │ with an id, a prompt in both languages, an input type, and a function   │
     │ that picks the next                                                     │
     │ node from the answers collected so far. This matters for three reasons: │
     │ it is testable, it                                                      │
     │ cannot go off the rails in front of a judge, and it costs nothing to    │
     │ run. An LLM driven                                                      │
     │ interview will hallucinate a step at the worst possible moment.         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The flow:                                                               │
     │                                                                         │
     │ 1. What went wrong. One large free text field, voice input available,   │
     │ Hindi or English.                                                       │
     │ This is the only open ended question in the whole interview and it does │
     │ the most work,                                                          │
     │ because runRemedyTriage() and runJurisdictionTriage() in src/lib/ both  │
     │ take raw text.                                                          │
     │ 2. Run the two triages immediately and show the citizen what was worked │
     │ out, in plain                                                           │
     │ language, before asking anything else. "This looks like a land record   │
     │ question. Land is a                                                     │
     │ State subject under List II Entry 18 of the Constitution, so a Central  │
     │ office cannot answer                                                    │
     │ it and would send it back." That single screen is the strongest thing   │
     │ this product does and                                                   │
     │ it should appear roughly forty seconds in.                              │
     │ 3. Branch on the remedy class. If runRemedyTriage() returns consumer,   │
     │ labour,                                                                 │
     │ tenancy or grievance, do not push an RTI. Tell the citizen the forum    │
     │ that can actually                                                       │
     │ give them the relief, with the limitation period, which                 │
     │ src/lib/remedy.ts already                                               │
     │ computes including consumer pecuniary jurisdiction. Then offer the RTI  │
     │ as a way to get the                                                     │
     │ record they will need as evidence. That refusal to draft an RTI when an │
     │ RTI is the wrong                                                        │
     │ instrument is the honesty that a sharp judge will notice.               │
     │ 4. Which state, only asked if the jurisdiction triage came back State   │
     │ or Concurrent.                                                          │
     │ 5. Pick the office, from the candidates the engine produced, each with  │
     │ its confidence and                                                      │
     │ its reasoning shown. If there are no candidates, say so and stop. Do    │
     │ not guess an address.                                                   │
     │ DIRECTORY_COVERAGE_STATEMENT in src/lib/data/authorities.ts is already  │
     │ written for this.                                                       │
     │ 6. Turn the problem into questions. Offer three or four question        │
     │ templates appropriate to                                                │
     │ the subject matter, each pre-filled and editable. Run lintQuestion()    │
     │ live as they edit,                                                      │
     │ the way QuestionComposer.tsx already does. When a rule fires, show the  │
     │ plain language                                                          │
     │ explanation and the mechanical rewrite, with the optional Gemini        │
     │ phrasing beside it, which                                               │
     │ is what PolishRewriteButton.tsx already does.                           │
     │ 7. Your details. Name, address, BPL status. Explain why each is needed  │
     │ and cite section                                                        │
     │ 6(2), which says no reason for the request need be given. Most citizens │
     │ do not know that.                                                       │
     │ 8. Fee. From lookupStateFee(), showing the confidence flag honestly     │
     │ when the source                                                         │
     │ conflicts. If BPL, show BPL_EXEMPTION_NOTE, which is a central          │
     │ provision no state rule                                                 │
     │ can override.                                                           │
     │ 9. Review and download. The PDF, plus the plain language copy in the    │
     │ citizen's language,                                                     │
     │ plus a clear instruction on where this goes: rtionline.gov.in for a     │
     │ Central authority,                                                      │
     │ the named state portal or postal address for a State authority.         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Gemini is allowed exactly two jobs in this interview: summarise the     │
     │ free text into                                                          │
     │ grievanceSummary, and phrase a rewrite. It decides nothing.             │
     │ src/lib/gemini/client.ts                                                │
     │ already has the three tier fallback chain and both features already     │
     │ degrade to a clear inline                                               │
     │ message when no key is present. Keep that. The interview must complete  │
     │ fully with                                                              │
     │ GEMINI_API_KEY unset, and there should be a test that proves it.        │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Test: engine.test.ts walks at least four full paths through the graph:  │
     │ a clean central                                                         │
     │ RTI, a state subject that produces the blocking warning, a consumer     │
     │ complaint that gets                                                     │
     │ diverted away from RTI, and a tenancy case that goes out of coverage.   │
     │ Assert the terminal                                                     │
     │ node and the resulting CaseRecord shape on each.                        │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ WP5: The Rights Navigator                                               │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The hardest package, and the one that will get attacked hardest, so     │
     │ build it defensively.                                                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The failure everyone else will ship is a chatbot with government PDFs   │
     │ stuffed into it that                                                    │
     │ answers confidently and wrongly. The research document names this exact │
     │ anti-pattern. Do not                                                    │
     │ build it.                                                               │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Build a curated corpus with deterministic retrieval and a phrasing-only │
     │ model layer.                                                            │
     │                                                                         │
     │ New files:                                                              │
     │ src/lib/data/rights-corpus.ts     CorpusChunk[], hand transcribed       │
     │ src/lib/data/corpus.ts            merges every corpus, single lookup    │
     │ src/lib/rights/retrieve.ts        deterministic scoring, pure function  │
     │ src/lib/rights/retrieve.test.ts                                         │
     │ src/lib/rights/answer.ts          builds the grounded answer            │
     │ src/app/rights/page.tsx                                                 │
     │ src/components/rights/RightsAsk.tsx                                     │
     │ Edited: src/lib/citations.ts                                            │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Corpus. Reuse the CorpusChunk type that already exists in               │
     │ src/lib/types.ts and the                                                │
     │ exact authoring style of src/lib/data/rti-act.ts. Read that file first. │
     │ Note how each chunk                                                     │
     │ carries act, section, heading, verbatim text, an optional note          │
     │ explaining the                                                          │
     │ practical effect, and a sourceUrl. Match it.                            │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Cover these, roughly forty to sixty chunks total:                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ - Constitution of India, the articles a citizen actually needs: 14,     │
     │ 19(1)(a), 21, 32, 226,                                                  │
     │ 300A, and Seventh Schedule List II Entries 18 and 2 and List III Entry  │
     │ 25, which the                                                           │
     │ jurisdiction engine already reasons about and can now cite properly.    │
     │ - Consumer Protection Act 2019: sections 2(7) consumer, 2(11)           │
     │ deficiency, 35 district                                                 │
     │ commission complaint, 69 two year limitation, and the pecuniary limits  │
     │ from the Consumer                                                       │
     │ Protection (Jurisdiction) Rules 2021, which src/lib/remedy.ts already   │
     │ hardcodes as fifty                                                      │
     │ lakh and two crore on price paid. Now those numbers get a citation.     │
     │ - Model Tenancy Act 2021: the security deposit cap, the Rent Authority, │
     │ notice periods.                                                         │
     │ Flag every chunk with a note saying this is a model law that a state    │
     │ must adopt, and name                                                    │
     │ which states have. That caveat is not optional. Getting it wrong is the │
     │ kind of mistake a                                                       │
     │ legally literate judge catches immediately.                             │
     │ - Code on Wages 2019 and Industrial Disputes Act 1947: timely payment   │
     │ of wages, the                                                           │
     │ authority to complain to, the limitation period, retrenchment notice.   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Source text from indiacode.nic.in and legislative.gov.in. Both are      │
     │ public domain. Note                                                     │
     │ that indiacode.nic.in returned 403 to an automated fetch during         │
     │ planning, so open it in a                                               │
     │ browser or use curl with a normal user agent. Transcribe. Do not        │
     │ paraphrase statutory text                                               │
     │ into a chunk's text field. Paraphrase belongs in note.                  │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Retrieval, and this is the whole trick. No vector database, no          │
     │ embeddings, no external                                                 │
     │ service. At this corpus size a plain lexical scorer beats an embedding  │
     │ pipeline on both                                                        │
     │ accuracy and honesty, and it is a pure function you can test.           │
     │                                                                         │
     │ scoreChunk(query, chunk) in retrieve.ts:                                │
     │ - Normalise both sides: lowercase, strip punctuation, drop a small      │
     │ English and Hindi stopword                                              │
     │ list.                                                                   │
     │ - Term frequency over text plus heading plus act, with heading weighted │
     │ higher.                                                                 │
     │ - Inverse document frequency across the corpus so common legal words    │
     │ like "section" and                                                      │
     │ "person" do not dominate.                                               │
     │ - A bonus for a matched multi-word phrase, so "security deposit" scores │
     │ above two loose hits                                                    │
     │ on "security" and "deposit".                                            │
     │ - Return the top five chunks with scores.                               │
     │                                                                         │
     │ RETRIEVAL_FLOOR, a named exported constant. If the top score falls      │
     │ below it, the Navigator                                                 │
     │ returns "I do not have a source for this" and offers what it can do     │
     │ instead, which is show                                                  │
     │ the nearest topics it does cover and offer to start an RTI. It does not │
     │ answer. Test this                                                       │
     │ path explicitly with an off-corpus question such as "how do I get a     │
     │ divorce". A judge will                                                  │
     │ try exactly that.                                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Answering. buildRightsAnswer() takes the retrieved chunks and           │
     │ constructs the answer.                                                  │
     │ The chunks are the answer. Gemini is given the retrieved text and a     │
     │ strict instruction to                                                   │
     │ explain it in plain language without adding any fact not present in the │
     │ chunks, and its output                                                  │
     │ is rendered beside the statutory text, never instead of it. If no       │
     │ GEMINI_API_KEY is present,                                              │
     │ the app shows the chunks with their note fields, which is a worse read  │
     │ but still a correct                                                     │
     │ answer. Test that path too.                                             │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The citation gate. src/lib/citations.ts currently imports               │
     │ getCorpusChunk from                                                     │
     │ rti-act.ts. Create src/lib/data/corpus.ts exporting a getCorpusChunk    │
     │ that searches both                                                      │
     │ RTI_ACT_CORPUS and RIGHTS_CORPUS, and repoint citations.ts at it.       │
     │ Everything downstream                                                   │
     │ keeps working, and the existing rule holds: a legal claim whose         │
     │ citation does not resolve                                               │
     │ throws rather than renders. That gate is already tested in              │
     │ citations.test.ts. Extend the                                           │
     │ test to cover a rights corpus id.                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Every answer carries a boundary line. Not in the footer. Attached to    │
     │ the answer: this                                                        │
     │ explains what the law says, it is not advice about your specific case,  │
     │ and for that you need a                                                 │
     │ lawyer or a legal aid clinic. Never output a directive like "you should │
     │ sue".                                                                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The handoff. Under every answer, a button: "Get the record you will     │
     │ need". It carries the                                                   │
     │ question text into /start with the interview pre-seeded. This is the    │
     │ single most important                                                   │
     │ button in the build. It is the thing the two minute video shows.        │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ WP6: The Scheme Eligibility Reader                                      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Bounded on purpose, and the boundary is stated in the interface.        │
     │                                                                         │
     │ New files:                                                              │
     │ src/lib/data/schemes.ts           the scheme knowledge base             │
     │ src/lib/schemes/evaluate.ts       deterministic evaluator               │
     │ src/lib/schemes/evaluate.test.ts                                        │
     │ src/app/schemes/page.tsx                                                │
     │ src/components/schemes/EligibilityWizard.tsx                            │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Eight schemes, hand verified. Suggested set, chosen because between     │
     │ them they cover most                                                    │
     │ of what an ordinary household asks about: PM-KISAN, Ayushman Bharat     │
     │ PM-JAY, PM Awas Yojana                                                  │
     │ Gramin, PM Ujjwala Yojana, the National Social Assistance Programme old │
     │ age pension, Sukanya                                                    │
     │ Samriddhi, PM Vishwakarma, and one state scheme so the Union and State  │
     │ distinction shows up                                                    │
     │ here too.                                                               │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Verify each one against its own official portal before writing it down. │
     │ Not against a                                                           │
     │ blog, not against a comparison site, not from memory. During planning   │
     │ the third party sources                                                 │
     │ for these disagreed with each other on income thresholds, which is      │
     │ precisely why this rule                                                 │
     │ exists. Each scheme record carries:                                     │
     │                                                                         │
     │ {                                                                       │
     │   id, name, nameHi, ministry,                                           │
     │   criteria: Criterion[],       // each with id, label, labelHi, type,   │
     │ and a pure predicate                                                    │
     │   benefit, benefitHi,                                                   │
     │   applyUrl,                                                             │
     │   sourceUrl,                   // the official page you actually read   │
     │   verifiedOn: "2026-08-XX",    // the date you read it                  │
     │   confidence: "verified" | "conflicting_sources" | "unverified",        │
     │   note?: string                // what disagreed, if anything           │
     │ }                                                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ If two official pages disagree, mark it conflicting_sources, write what │
     │ disagreed in note,                                                      │
     │ and show that in the interface. That behaviour already exists for the   │
     │ Maharashtra RTI fee in                                                  │
     │ state-fees.ts. It reads as rigour, not as weakness.                     │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The evaluator decides. No model touches it. evaluateScheme(profile,     │
     │ scheme) returns per                                                     │
     │ criterion: met, not_met, or unknown, with the criterion's own text as   │
     │ the reason. Scheme                                                      │
     │ level result is eligible only when every criterion is met, not_eligible │
     │ when any is                                                             │
     │ not_met, and more_information_needed otherwise. Never guess an unknown  │
     │ into a met.                                                             │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The interface. Ask the smallest set of profile questions that can       │
     │ discriminate across all                                                 │
     │ eight schemes, roughly six to eight: age, state, annual household       │
     │ income band, land holding,                                              │
     │ ration card type, occupation, whether a pucca house is owned, gender of │
     │ the child for Sukanya.                                                  │
     │ Then show all eight, sorted eligible first, each with the criteria it   │
     │ passed and failed spelled                                               │
     │ out. Do not show a bare yes or no. Showing the working is the whole     │
     │ point, because a citizen                                                │
     │ who sees "you fail on land holding" learns something a yes or no never  │
     │ teaches.                                                                │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Name the incumbent. myscheme.gov.in exists and is the government's own  │
     │ scheme discovery                                                        │
     │ portal. Say so on the page, and say what is different here: this one    │
     │ shows the rule it applied                                               │
     │ and hands you a way to challenge the answer in writing. Pretending      │
     │ myScheme does not exist is                                              │
     │ the single easiest way to lose a technical judge.                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The handoff. On a not_eligible or more_information_needed result: "Ask  │
     │ them why, in                                                            │
     │ writing". It seeds /start with a question along the lines of asking the │
     │ department for a                                                        │
     │ certified copy of the criteria applied and the reason for rejection.    │
     │ That routes straight                                                    │
     │ into the jurisdiction engine, which will correctly work out that most   │
     │ of these schemes are                                                    │
     │ delivered by State departments even when the scheme is Central. That is │
     │ a genuinely                                                             │
     │ interesting thing for the app to know and it falls out of code that     │
     │ already exists.                                                         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ WP7: Hindi and voice                                                    │
     │                                                                         │
     │ New files:                                                              │
     │ src/lib/i18n/en.ts                                                      │
     │ src/lib/i18n/hi.ts                                                      │
     │ src/lib/i18n/index.ts                                                   │
     │ src/components/LanguageToggle.tsx                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ A flat key to string dictionary and a t(key) function. No i18n library. │
     │ The surface is a few                                                    │
     │ hundred strings and a library brings a routing model this app does not  │
     │ need.                                                                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Language choice lives in a cookie so it survives a reload and is        │
     │ readable in Server Components                                           │
     │ without a client round trip. The toggle sits in SiteHeader.tsx, visible │
     │ on every page.                                                          │
     │                                                                         │
     │ When the interface is in Hindi, apply var(--font-devanagari), which is  │
     │ already wired to                                                        │
     │ Tiro_Devanagari_Hindi in layout.tsx. Latin numerals throughout,         │
     │ including in Hindi, because                                             │
     │ that is what Indian government forms and portals use.                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Translation honesty. Translate the interface by hand. Do not machine    │
     │ translate the UI. But                                                   │
     │ statutory text stays in English with a Hindi explanation beside it,     │
     │ never a Hindi replacement.                                              │
     │ Section 6(1) permits an application in English, Hindi or the local      │
     │ official language, and the                                              │
     │ generated PDF must stay in the language it will actually be filed in. A │
     │ Hindi translation of a                                                  │
     │ statutory quotation shown as if it were the statute is a real defect,   │
     │ not a nicety.                                                           │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Voice. VoiceDictationButton.tsx exists. Read it, extend it to take a    │
     │ lang prop, pass                                                         │
     │ hi-IN when the interface is Hindi. Use it on the free text step of the  │
     │ interview and on the                                                    │
     │ Rights Navigator question box. Where the Web Speech API is unavailable, │
     │ hide the button rather                                                  │
     │ than showing a broken one, and make sure typing always works.           │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ WP8: The clock that actually runs                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The current sweep runs when someone clicks a button labelled "demo      │
     │ control". A judge will read                                             │
     │ that as fake, and they will be right.                                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ New files: src/app/api/cron/sweep/route.ts, vercel.json                 │
     │ Edited: src/lib/sweep.ts if needed, src/app/my/[id]/page.tsx            │
     │                                                                         │
     │ - A route handler that loads every case with an open deadline, runs     │
     │ sweepCase(), and persists                                               │
     │ the results. Guard it with a CRON_SECRET bearer token so it is not a    │
     │ public endpoint.                                                        │
     │ - vercel.json with a daily cron schedule pointing at it. Vercel's Hobby │
     │ plan allows daily                                                       │
     │ crons, which is enough.                                                 │
     │ - Keep the manual trigger, but reframe it. It stops being a demo        │
     │ control and becomes a                                                   │
     │ deliberate feature: "Show me what happens if they miss the deadline",   │
     │ which simulates a                                                       │
     │ future date and shows the citizen the first appeal that will be drafted │
     │ for them. That is a                                                     │
     │ genuinely reassuring thing for a citizen to be able to see on day two,  │
     │ and it doubles as the                                                   │
     │ demo without pretending to be something else.                           │
     │ - On /my, a filing whose deadline has lapsed gets a distinct visual     │
     │ state and its drafted                                                   │
     │ first appeal is one click away.                                         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Check: curl the cron route with and without the secret. Confirm a       │
     │ seeded overdue case                                                     │
     │ transitions to deemed_refusal with a drafted appeal in operatorNotes,   │
     │ which WP3 renamed.                                                      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ WP9: Seeded demo data                                                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ A judge signing in must land on something alive. An empty dashboard is  │
     │ a dead demo.                                                            │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Edited: scripts/seed-cases.ts                                           │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The script already exists and already paces its Firestore writes to     │
     │ respect the rate limiter,                                               │
     │ which was a real bug someone already fixed. Extend it, do not rewrite   │
     │ it. It should create the                                                │
     │ demo account's cases in these states:                                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ 1. A land record case, filed eight days ago, twenty two days left on    │
     │ the clock. Shows a live                                                 │
     │ countdown.                                                              │
     │ 2. A case where the citizen picked a Central ministry for a State       │
     │ subject and the blocking                                                │
     │ warning fired. Shows the engine catching a defect before filing.        │
     │ 3. An overdue case, deemed refusal, first appeal already drafted and    │
     │ waiting. Shows the                                                      │
     │ moneyshot without needing anyone to click anything.                     │
     │ 4. A consumer complaint that was correctly diverted away from RTI to    │
     │ the Consumer Commission                                                 │
     │ with the pecuniary jurisdiction computed. Shows the tool refusing to    │
     │ file the wrong thing.                                                   │
     │ 5. A fresh draft, mid interview, so "resume where you left off" is      │
     │ visible.                                                                │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Make the grievance text read like a real person wrote it. Look at how   │
     │ the DoLR register                                                       │
     │ entries are phrased and match that register. Do not write specimen text │
     │ that sounds like a                                                      │
     │ product manager.                                                        │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Realistic names and Indian addresses. No real people. No real Aadhaar   │
     │ or PAN numbers anywhere,                                                │
     │ including in placeholder text, because the RTI portal's own guidelines  │
     │ specifically warn                                                       │
     │ against uploading those.                                                │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ WP10: Landing page and the pitch surface                                │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The landing page currently argues to a hackathon judge that the         │
     │ jurisdiction engine is                                                  │
     │ rigorous. Keep the rigour, change the audience. It now has to do two    │
     │ jobs at once: convince a                                                │
     │ Build What Moves India judge in ninety seconds, and get an actual       │
     │ citizen to press start.                                                 │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Keep the existing six act structure in src/components/landing/. Keep    │
     │ the evidence numeral                                                    │
     │ moment, which is already the strongest thing on the page. Change:       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ - The headline becomes the citizen's problem, not the engine's          │
     │ capability.                                                             │
     │ - Add one act, early, that names the incumbent honestly and shows the   │
     │ specific gap. Two                                                       │
     │ columns: what rtionline.gov.in does, what happens next. No registration │
     │ number to lose. A                                                       │
     │ clock that runs itself. An answer that tells you which government owns  │
     │ your problem before                                                     │
     │ you pay ten rupees. Do not sneer at the portal. It works, it is         │
     │ genuinely useful, and the                                               │
     │ respectful framing is both truer and more persuasive.                   │
     │ - The four features shown as one journey with the handoff arrows drawn, │
     │ not as a feature grid.                                                  │
     │ - A single primary call to action, "Start with what went wrong",        │
     │ straight into /start.                                                   │
     │ - Demo credentials visible in the footer of the landing page too, not   │
     │ only on /login.                                                         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Update README.md: the positioning, the four features, the auth section, │
     │ the demo                                                                │
     │ credentials, the new route table, the test count once it changes. The   │
     │ README is currently very                                                │
     │ good and several judges will read it. Do not let it drift out of date,  │
     │ and do not let it                                                       │
     │ overclaim. Every honesty caveat that is in it now stays in it.          │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ WP11: Verification                                                      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Nothing is done until this passes.                                      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ 1. npm run verify, which is lint then typecheck then test then build.   │
     │ All green.                                                              │
     │ 2. Test count is higher than 109 and the number in the README badge     │
     │ matches reality.                                                        │
     │ 3. npm run eval still reports the same DoLR gold set numbers. The       │
     │ jurisdiction classifier was                                             │
     │ not supposed to change. If those numbers moved, something got broken.   │
     │ 4. Run the whole thing with GEMINI_API_KEY unset and confirm every one  │
     │ of the four features                                                    │
     │ still completes. This is the test that proves the AI layer is optional  │
     │ rather than                                                             │
     │ load-bearing, and it is the answer to the "is this just a ChatGPT       │
     │ wrapper" question that                                                  │
     │ will definitely be asked.                                               │
     │ 5. Run it with the FIREBASE_* variables unset and confirm the file      │
     │ store fallback still                                                    │
     │ works, with the exception that auth will be unavailable, which should   │
     │ produce a clear                                                         │
     │ message rather than a crash.                                            │
     │ 6. Manual pass on the full flow at 1440px and at 390px, in both         │
     │ languages: sign in with the                                             │
     │ demo button, complete the interview end to end, download the PDF and    │
     │ open it, ask the Rights                                                 │
     │ Navigator something in the corpus, ask it something outside the corpus  │
     │ and confirm it                                                          │
     │ declines, run the scheme checker, use both handoff buttons, watch a     │
     │ deadline lapse and the                                                  │
     │ appeal appear.                                                          │
     │ 7. Zero console errors on every route.                                  │
     │ 8. Grep every changed file for em dash and en dash bytes. Must return   │
     │ nothing. This is a                                                      │
     │ standing rule for this project and it applies to code comments and the  │
     │ README as well as to                                                    │
     │ user-visible copy.                                                      │
     │ 9. Deploy to Vercel, set every new environment variable, and repeat     │
     │ step 6 against the live                                                 │
     │ URL. Confirm the demo credentials work on the deployed instance from a  │
     │ fresh browser                                                           │
     │ profile with no cached session.                                         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ For the submission itself                                               │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Four things go on the form. Have these ready before the final deploy,   │
     │ not after.                                                              │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The live link. The Vercel URL, with demo credentials both on the login  │
     │ page and in the                                                         │
     │ form's text summary.                                                    │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The two minute video. First minute is the flow, no narration about      │
     │ architecture: describe                                                  │
     │ a problem in Hindi by voice, watch the app work out that it is a State  │
     │ subject and that a                                                      │
     │ Central office would send it back, get the drafted application, then    │
     │ jump forward and watch                                                  │
     │ the first appeal write itself. Second minute is why it was built this   │
     │ way: the deterministic                                                  │
     │ core decides, the model only phrases, the retrieval declines to answer  │
     │ when it has no source,                                                  │
     │ and every legal claim on screen resolves to real statutory text or it   │
     │ does not render at all.                                                 │
     │ Show the citation popover. Show the "I do not have a source for this"   │
     │ state deliberately. A                                                   │
     │ tool that admits a gap is more convincing than one that never does.     │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The 250 word summary. One sharp claim, not a feature list. The claim is │
     │ the continuous                                                          │
     │ journey: four things a citizen needs and four separate tools that each  │
     │ answer one and hand you                                                 │
     │ back to yourself. Then the number, which is 2,80,353 applications       │
     │ transferred between                                                     │
     │ authorities in 2024-25 and 2,797 of 3,128 returned unanswered by one    │
     │ department. Then the                                                    │
     │ scope boundary, stated plainly, because the honesty is part of the      │
     │ pitch: it drafts, it never                                              │
     │ submits, it explains the law and never advises on your case, and it     │
     │ covers twelve central                                                   │
     │ departments, two states and eight schemes by name rather than claiming  │
     │ the country.                                                            │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Partner email. Both partners have to enter each other's registered      │
     │ address, and it has to                                                  │
     │ be the same address used at registration. There is no recovery path if  │
     │ it is wrong.                                                            │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ Rules that hold across every package                                    │
     │                                                                         │
     │ - No em dashes and no en dashes anywhere, including code comments.      │
     │ Commas, colons, full stops,                                             │
     │ parentheses.                                                            │
     │ - No number renders in the interface without a source next to it or     │
     │ behind a citation tag.                                                  │
     │ - The model may propose and phrase. It may never adjudicate or compute. │
     │ If you find yourself                                                    │
     │ about to let Gemini decide a jurisdiction, an eligibility, a deadline   │
     │ or a fee, stop. That is                                                 │
     │ the line this whole product is built on.                                │
     │ - When a source cannot be confirmed, mark it unverified and show the    │
     │ mark. Do not round it into                                              │
     │ confidence.                                                             │
     │ - Do not remove an honesty caveat to make a screen read cleaner. The    │
     │ caveats are the product.                                                │
     │ - Test the failure path, not only the happy path. The Rights Navigator  │
     │ declining to answer, the                                                │
     │ jurisdiction engine refusing to name an authority it does not cover,    │
     │ the interview diverting                                                 │
     │ away from RTI: all three are features, and all three need a test.       │
