     │ record.                                                                 │
     │                                                                         │
     │ SiteHeader.tsx becomes an async Server Component that reads             │
     │ getSession() and shows either                                           │
     │ the nav plus a sign out button, or a sign in link.                      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Check:                                                                  │
     │ - Demo button signs in and lands on /my.                                │
     │ - Sign out, then sign in with a fresh Google account: /my shows an      │
     │ empty state, not somebody                                               │
     │ else's cases.                                                           │
     │ - While signed in as the fresh account, open a case id belonging to the │
     │ demo account. Expect a                                                  │
     │ 404 page, not an error page and not the case.                           │
     │ - Visit /my signed out: redirected to /login?next=/my, and after        │
     │ signing in you land back                                                │
     │ on /my.                                                                 │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Commit: Add citizen accounts, sessions, and a one-click demo login      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ WP3: The citizen shell                                                  │
     │                                                                         │
     │ "Docket" is a court clerk's word. "Operator" is a call centre word.     │
     │ Both are wrong for somebody                                             │
     │ trying to find out why their pension has not arrived.                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Route moves:                                                            │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ┌──────────────┬──────────┬─────────────────────────────┐               │
     │ │     From     │    To    │           Meaning           │               │
     │ ├──────────────┼──────────┼─────────────────────────────┤               │
     │ │ /docket      │ /my      │ My filings                  │               │
     │ ├──────────────┼──────────┼─────────────────────────────┤               │
     │ │ /docket/[id] │ /my/[id] │ One filing                  │               │
     │ ├──────────────┼──────────┼─────────────────────────────┤               │
     │ │ /docket/new  │ /start   │ The interview, built in WP4 │               │
     │ └──────────────┴──────────┴─────────────────────────────┘               │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Add permanent redirects in next.config.ts so any shared link still      │
     │ works:                                                                  │
     │ async redirects() {                                                     │
     │   return [                                                              │
     │     { source: "/docket", destination: "/my", permanent: true },         │
     │     { source: "/docket/new", destination: "/start", permanent: true },  │
     │     { source: "/docket/:id", destination: "/my/:id", permanent: true }, │
     │   ];                                                                    │
     │ }                                                                       │
     │ Order matters: /docket/new must come before /docket/:id or the dynamic  │
     │ rule swallows it.                                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Copy changes throughout. This is not cosmetic.                          │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ┌───────────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────── │
     │ ───┐                                                                    │
     │ │                Old                │                 New               │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ ├───────────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────── │
     │ ───┤                                                                    │
     │ │ Operator notes                    │ My notes                          │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ ├───────────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────── │
     │ ───┤                                                                    │
     │ │ Case / docket                     │ Filing, or "your application"     │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ ├───────────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────── │
     │ ───┤                                                                    │
     │ │ Is this the right instrument?     │ Will an RTI actually get you what │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ │                                   │ you need?                         │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ ├───────────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────── │
     │ ───┤                                                                    │
     │ │ Which government, and which       │ Who is legally required to answer │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ │ office?                           │ you                               │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ ├───────────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────── │
     │ ───┤                                                                    │
     │ │ Statutory clock                   │ Your deadline                     │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ ├───────────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────── │
     │ ───┤                                                                    │
     │ │ Mark filed and start the clock    │ I have sent it. Start my thirty   │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ │                                   │ days.                             │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ ├───────────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────── │
     │ ───┤                                                                    │
     │ │ Run the daily sweep manually      │ Show me what happens if they miss │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ │ (demo control)                    │ the deadline                      │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ ├───────────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────── │
     │ ───┤                                                                    │
     │ │ Export                            │ Download your application         │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ ├───────────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────── │
     │ ───┤                                                                    │
     │ │ Confirm authority                 │ This is the office I will write   │
     │ to  │                                                                   │
     │ ├───────────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────── │
     │ ───┤                                                                    │
     │ │ No cases yet                      │ You have not filed anything yet   │
     │   │                                                                     │
     │ └───────────────────────────────────┴────────────────────────────────── │
     │ ───┘                                                                    │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Restructure /my/[id]. It keeps every capability it has today. It        │
     │ changes shape. Today it                                                 │
     │ is nine stacked sections of equal visual weight, which is a workbench.  │
     │ Make it:                                                                │
     │                                                                         │
     │ - A status sentence at the top. One sentence saying where this filing   │
     │ stands and what the                                                     │
     │ citizen should do next. If there is nothing to do, say that plainly.    │
     │ Derive it from                                                          │
     │ caseRecord.status plus the earliest pending deadline. This is the       │
     │ single most useful thing                                                │
     │ on the page for a real user.                                            │
     │ - A deadline card, prominent. Days remaining as a large numeral using   │
     │ --step-5, the                                                           │
     │ section it comes from with a CitationTag, and one line on what happens  │
     │ at zero. When the                                                       │
     │ deadline has lapsed the card turns and surfaces the drafted first       │
     │ appeal, ready to copy.                                                  │
     │ - The application itself. Questions numbered, linter findings inline as │
     │ they are now.                                                           │
     │ - Everything else (fee, authority reasoning, notes, plain language      │
     │ copy) in a secondary                                                    │
     │ column at desktop width or behind disclosure on mobile.                 │
     │                                                                         │
     │ /my list page: keep the ledger table, add the empty state copy above,   │
     │ add an "overdue"                                                        │
     │ row treatment using --brick, and make the primary action "Start a new   │
     │ filing" pointing at                                                     │
     │ /start.                                                                 │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Check: every route renders. /docket and /docket/abc redirect. Then run  │
     │ grep -rin "operator\|docket" src/app src/components --include=*.tsx and │
     │ confirm every                                                           │
     │ remaining hit is a code identifier or a route redirect, never           │
     │ user-visible copy. operatorNotes                                        │
     │ as a field name on CaseRecord is fine to leave, renaming it churns the  │
     │ store for no benefit.                                                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Commit: Rebuild the shell around the citizen rather than the operator   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ WP4: The Conversational Form Filler                                     │
     │                                                                         │
     │ This replaces the one-page intake form with an interview. It is what    │
     │ turns "I have to                                                        │
     │ understand RTI to use this" into "I have to describe my problem".       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ New files:                                                              │
     │ src/lib/interview/graph.ts                                              │
     │ src/lib/interview/engine.ts                                             │
     │ src/lib/interview/engine.test.ts                                        │
     │ src/app/start/page.tsx                                                  │
     │ src/components/interview/Interview.tsx                                  │
     │ src/components/interview/StepShell.tsx                                  │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The graph shape:                                                        │
     │ export type StepId =                                                    │
     │   | "problem" | "triage_result" | "wrong_instrument" | "state"          │
     │   | "authority" | "questions" | "applicant" | "fee" | "review" |        │
     │ "out_of_coverage";                                                      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ export interface Answers {                                              │
     │   problem?: string;                                                     │
     │   state?: string;                                                       │
     │   authorityId?: string;                                                 │
     │   questions?: string[];                                                 │
     │   name?: string;                                                        │
     │   address?: string;                                                     │
     │   isBpl?: boolean;                                                      │
     │   preferredLanguage?: string;                                           │
     │   acknowledgedWrongInstrument?: boolean;                                │
     │ }                                                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ export interface Step {                                                 │
     │   id: StepId;                                                           │
     │   titleEn: string;                                                      │
     │   titleHi: string;                                                      │
     │   helpEn: string;                                                       │
     │   helpHi: string;                                                       │
     │   kind: "freetext" | "choice" | "questions" | "fields" | "result" |     │
     │ "terminal";                                                             │
     │ }                                                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ /** Pure. Given everything answered so far plus the two triage results, │
     │  *  return the next step id or null when the interview is complete.     │
     │  *  No side effects, no store access, no model call. */                 │
     │ export function nextStep(                                               │
     │   current: StepId,                                                      │
     │   answers: Answers,                                                     │
     │   jurisdiction: JurisdictionTriageResult,                               │
     │   remedy: RemedyTriageResult                                            │
     │ ): StepId | null;                                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The flow, step by step:                                                 │
     │                                                                         │
     │ 1. problem. One large free text field, dictation available via the      │
     │ existing                                                                │
     │ VoiceDictationButton, placeholder in the citizen's language. This is    │
     │ the only open ended                                                     │
     │ question in the whole interview and it does the most work, because both │
     │ runRemedyTriage(text) and runJurisdictionTriage({ grievanceText: text   │
     │ }) take raw text.                                                       │
     │ 2. triage_result. Run both triages immediately and show the citizen     │
     │ what was worked out,                                                    │
     │ in plain language, before asking anything else. Something like: "This   │
     │ looks like a land                                                       │
     │ record question. Land is a State subject under List II Entry 18 of the  │
     │ Constitution, so a                                                      │
     │ Central office cannot answer it and would send it back to you without   │
     │ refunding the fee."                                                     │
     │ Every clause there is already computed by runJurisdictionTriage:        │
     │ subjectMatter,                                                          │
     │ scheduleEntry, scheduleList, and blockingWarning.message. Render        │
     │ blockingWarning.citationId through CitationTag.                         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ 2. This screen is the strongest thing the product does and it should    │
     │ appear about forty                                                      │
     │ seconds in. Design it as the moment, not as a status line.              │
     │ 3. wrong_instrument, reached only when remedy.remedyClass is not rti.   │
     │ Do not push an                                                          │
     │ RTI. Show the forum that can actually give relief, from                 │
     │ remedy.forumName, with                                                  │
     │ remedy.limitationPeriod, and for consumer cases                         │
     │ remedy.pecuniaryJurisdiction.level and                                  │
     │ its reasoning, all of which src/lib/remedy.ts already computes. Then    │
     │ offer the RTI as a                                                      │
     │ way to obtain the record they will need as evidence, which is exactly   │
     │ what the hybrid                                                         │
     │ class is for. If remedy.outOfCoverage is true, which happens for        │
     │ tenancy, go to                                                          │
     │ out_of_coverage and stop, pointing at the State Rent Authority or a     │
     │ legal aid clinic.                                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ 3. This refusal to draft an RTI when an RTI is the wrong instrument is  │
     │ the honesty a sharp                                                     │
     │ judge notices. Do not soften it into a suggestion.                      │
     │ 4. state, asked only when jurisdiction.scheduleList is State or         │
     │ Concurrent. Options                                                     │
     │ come from STATE_FEES plus "somewhere else".                             │
     │ 5. authority. Show jurisdiction.candidates, each with its confidence as │
     │ a percentage,                                                           │
     │ its reasoning, its cpioAddress and its coverage note. If                │
     │ candidates.length === 0, show                                           │
     │ DIRECTORY_COVERAGE_STATEMENT from authorities.ts and go to              │
     │ out_of_coverage. Never                                                  │
     │ guess an address.                                                       │
     │ 6. questions. Offer three or four templates appropriate to the subject  │
     │ matter, each                                                            │
     │ pre-filled and editable. Run lintQuestion() on every keystroke exactly  │
     │ as                                                                      │
     │ QuestionComposer.tsx already does. When a rule fires, show its plain    │
     │ language explanation,                                                   │
     │ its CitationTag, and its mechanical rewrite with an accept button, plus │
     │ the optional Gemini                                                     │
     │ phrasing beside it via the existing polishRewriteAction.                │
     │                                                                         │
     │ 6. Seed the templates from SEVENTH_SCHEDULE_MAP[].subjectMatter. For    │
     │ land: a certified copy of                                               │
     │ the mutation register entry, the current 7/12 or khasra record, the     │
     │ file noting recording a                                                 │
     │ decision, the date an application was received. Write them so they      │
     │ already pass the linter,                                                │
     │ which teaches the citizen what a good question looks like.              │
     │ 7. applicant. Name, address, BPL. Explain why each is needed, and cite  │
     │ section 6(2) with a                                                     │
     │ CitationTag to say no reason for the request need be given. Most        │
     │ citizens do not know that                                               │
     │ and it is a small delight to learn it.                                  │
     │ 8. fee. From lookupStateFee(state) or CENTRAL_FEE, showing the          │
     │ confidence flag                                                         │
     │ honestly when sources conflict, which they do for Maharashtra. If BPL,  │
     │ show                                                                    │
     │ BPL_EXEMPTION_NOTE and set fee.waived.                                  │
     │ 9. review. The finished application, the PDF download, the plain        │
     │ language copy in the                                                    │
     │ citizen's language, and a clear instruction on where this goes:         │
     │ rtionline.gov.in for a                                                  │
     │ Central authority, the named state portal or the postal address for a   │
     │ State authority.                                                        │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Gemini gets exactly two jobs in this interview: summarise the free text │
     │ into                                                                    │
     │ grievanceSummary, and phrase a rewrite. It decides nothing. The         │
     │ interview must complete                                                 │
     │ fully with GEMINI_API_KEY unset, and there must be a test proving it.   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Test, engine.test.ts: walk at least four complete paths and assert the  │
     │ terminal step and                                                       │
     │ the resulting CaseRecord shape on each.                                 │
     │ - a clean Central RTI, for example a passport delay, ending at review   │
     │ - a State subject that fires blockingWarning, asserting the warning is  │
     │ present                                                                 │
     │ - a consumer complaint, asserting it routes through wrong_instrument    │
     │ with a                                                                  │
     │ pecuniaryJurisdiction computed                                          │
     │ - a tenancy case, asserting it terminates at out_of_coverage            │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Check: complete the interview end to end in the browser with            │
     │ GEMINI_API_KEY unset, then                                              │
     │ again with it set, and confirm both produce a downloadable PDF that     │
     │ opens.                                                                  │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Commit: Replace the intake form with a guided interview                 │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ WP5: The Rights Navigator                                               │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The largest new piece and the one that gets attacked hardest. Build it  │
     │ defensively.                                                            │
     │                                                                         │
     │ New files:                                                              │
     │ src/lib/data/rights-corpus.ts                                           │
     │ src/lib/data/corpus.ts                                                  │
     │ src/lib/rights/retrieve.ts                                              │
     │ src/lib/rights/retrieve.test.ts                                         │
     │ src/lib/rights/answer.ts                                                │
     │ src/app/rights/page.tsx                                                 │
     │ src/components/rights/RightsAsk.tsx                                     │
     │ Edited: src/lib/citations.ts, src/lib/citations.test.ts                 │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The corpus. Reuse the CorpusChunk type already in src/lib/types.ts and  │
     │ copy the                                                                │
     │ authoring style of src/lib/data/rti-act.ts exactly. Read that file      │
     │ first. Note how each chunk                                              │
     │ carries act, section, heading, verbatim text, an optional note          │
     │ explaining the                                                          │
     │ practical effect, and a sourceUrl. Note how the file's header comment   │
     │ records what was                                                        │
     │ cleaned from the source and why 8(1)(j) is stored in two versions.      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Forty to sixty chunks covering:                                         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ - Constitution of India: Articles 14, 19(1)(a), 21, 32, 226, 300A, plus │
     │ Seventh Schedule                                                        │
     │ List II Entries 2 and 18 and List III Entry 25. Those Schedule entries  │
     │ matter because                                                          │
     │ src/lib/data/seventh-schedule.ts already reasons about them and can now │
     │ cite them properly.                                                     │
     │ - Consumer Protection Act 2019: sections 2(7) consumer, 2(11)           │
     │ deficiency, 35 complaint to                                             │
     │ the District Commission, 69 two year limitation. Plus the pecuniary     │
     │ limits from the Consumer                                                │
     │ Protection (Jurisdiction of the District, State and National            │
     │ Commission) Rules 2021, which                                           │
     │ src/lib/remedy.ts currently hardcodes as fifty lakh and two crore on    │
     │ price paid. Those                                                       │
     │ numbers get a citation for the first time.                              │
     │ - Model Tenancy Act 2021: security deposit cap, the Rent Authority,     │
     │ notice periods. Every                                                   │
     │ chunk here carries a note saying this is a model law a state must       │
     │ adopt, and naming which                                                 │
     │ states have. That caveat is not optional. Getting it wrong is exactly   │
     │ the mistake a legally                                                   │
     │ literate judge catches.                                                 │
     │ - Code on Wages 2019 and Industrial Disputes Act 1947: timely payment   │
     │ of wages, the                                                           │
     │ authority to complain to, the limitation period, retrenchment notice.   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Source from indiacode.nic.in and legislative.gov.in, both public        │
     │ domain. Note that                                                       │
     │ indiacode.nic.in returned HTTP 403 to an automated fetch during         │
     │ planning, so use curl with a                                            │
     │ normal browser user agent, or open it and copy. Transcribe. Do not      │
     │ paraphrase statutory text                                               │
     │ into text. Paraphrase belongs in note.                                  │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Record in RESEARCH.md which documents you actually opened and on what   │
     │ date.                                                                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The merged corpus. Create src/lib/data/corpus.ts:                       │
     │ import { RTI_ACT_CORPUS } from "@/lib/data/rti-act";                    │
     │ import { RIGHTS_CORPUS } from "@/lib/data/rights-corpus";               │
     │ import type { CorpusChunk } from "@/lib/types";                         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ export const ALL_CORPUS: CorpusChunk[] = [...RTI_ACT_CORPUS,            │
     │ ...RIGHTS_CORPUS];                                                      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ export function getCorpusChunk(id: string): CorpusChunk | undefined {   │
     │   return ALL_CORPUS.find((c) => c.id === id);                           │
     │ }                                                                       │
     │ Repoint the import in src/lib/citations.ts at this. Everything          │
     │ downstream keeps working and                                            │
     │ the render gate still throws on an unresolved id. Leave getCorpusChunk  │
     │ in rti-act.ts in                                                        │
     │ place so existing imports do not break, and add a test asserting no two │
     │ chunks across the merged                                                │
     │ corpus share an id, because a duplicate id would silently shadow one of │
     │ them.                                                                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Retrieval. src/lib/rights/retrieve.ts, pure functions, no I/O:          │
     │                                                                         │
     │ export const RETRIEVAL_FLOOR = 0.18; // tune against the test set, then │
     │ freeze                                                                  │
     │                                                                         │
     │ export interface Scored { chunk: CorpusChunk; score: number; }          │
     │                                                                         │
     │ export function retrieve(query: string, corpus = ALL_CORPUS): Scored[]; │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The scorer:                                                             │
     │ 1. Normalise both sides: lowercase, strip punctuation, collapse         │
     │ whitespace, drop a small                                                │
     │ stopword list. Include Hindi stopwords too, transliterated and          │
     │ Devanagari, because a citizen                                           │
     │ may type in either.                                                     │
     │ 2. Term frequency over heading + " " + act + " " + text, with terms     │
     │ matched in heading                                                      │
     │ weighted three times.                                                   │
     │ 3. Inverse document frequency across the corpus, so "section",          │
     │ "person", "provided" do not                                             │
     │ dominate.                                                               │
     │ 4. A phrase bonus: if a two or three word span of the query appears     │
     │ contiguously in the chunk,                                              │
     │ add a fixed bonus. This makes "security deposit" beat two loose hits on │
     │ "security" and                                                          │
     │ "deposit".                                                              │
     │ 5. Normalise the final score into 0 to 1 by dividing by the best        │
     │ possible score for that query,                                          │
     │ so RETRIEVAL_FLOOR means something stable.                              │
     │ 6. Return the top five sorted descending.                               │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The floor is the feature. If retrieve()[0].score < RETRIEVAL_FLOOR, the │
     │ Navigator does not                                                      │
     │ answer. It says it has no source for this, lists the topics it does     │
     │ cover, and offers to start                                              │
     │ an RTI instead.                                                         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Test, retrieve.test.ts: at least ten cases.                             │
     │ - Five in-corpus questions that must return the expected chunk id at    │
     │ rank one. For example "my                                               │
     │ landlord is keeping my security deposit" returns the Model Tenancy Act  │
     │ deposit chunk.                                                          │
     │ - Three off-corpus questions that must fall below the floor. Use "how   │
     │ do I get a divorce", "what                                              │
     │ is the capital of France", "how do I start a company". A judge will     │
     │ type one of these.                                                      │
     │ - One asserting the floor constant is exported and is between 0 and 1.  │
     │ - One asserting every returned chunk id resolves through                │
     │ getCorpusChunk.                                                         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Answering. src/lib/rights/answer.ts. The retrieved chunks are the       │
     │ answer. Gemini is given                                                 │
     │ the retrieved text and a system instruction in the same style as        │
     │ src/lib/gemini/translate.ts: numbered rules, explicit prohibitions,     │
     │ output only the text. Its                                               │
     │ job is to explain the retrieved chunks in plain language, adding no     │
     │ fact not present in them.                                               │
     │ Its output renders beside the statutory text, never instead of it.      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ With no GEMINI_API_KEY, show the chunks with their note fields. A worse │
     │ read, still a                                                           │
     │ correct answer. Test that path.                                         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Every answer carries its boundary line attached to it, not in the page  │
     │ footer: this explains                                                   │
     │ what the law says, it is not advice about your specific situation, and  │
     │ for that you need a                                                     │
     │ lawyer or a legal aid clinic. Never output a directive like "you should │
     │ sue". The system                                                        │
     │ instruction says so and the UI says so.                                 │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The handoff. Under every answer, a button: "Get the record you will     │
     │ need". It carries                                                       │
     │ the question into /start with the interview pre-seeded. This is the     │
     │ single most important                                                   │
     │ button in the build. It is the thing the two minute video shows.        │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Commit: Add the Rights Navigator, grounded in a cited statute corpus    │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ WP6: The Scheme Eligibility Reader                                      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Bounded on purpose, and the boundary is stated in the interface.        │
     │                                                                         │
     │ New files:                                                              │
     │ src/lib/data/schemes.ts                                                 │
     │ src/lib/schemes/evaluate.ts                                             │
     │ src/lib/schemes/evaluate.test.ts                                        │
     │ src/app/schemes/page.tsx                                                │
     │ src/components/schemes/EligibilityWizard.tsx                            │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Eight schemes. Suggested set, chosen because between them they cover    │
     │ most of what an                                                         │
     │ ordinary household asks about: PM-KISAN, Ayushman Bharat PM-JAY, PM     │
     │ Awas Yojana Gramin, PM                                                  │
     │ Ujjwala Yojana, the National Social Assistance Programme old age        │
     │ pension, Sukanya Samriddhi, PM                                          │
     │ Vishwakarma, and one state scheme so the Union and State distinction    │
     │ shows up here too.                                                      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Verify each against its own official portal. Not a blog, not a          │
     │ comparison site, not memory.                                            │
     │ During planning, third party sources disagreed with each other on       │
     │ income thresholds for several                                           │
     │ of these, which is precisely why this rule exists.                      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The record shape:                                                       │
     │ export type CriterionType = "age" | "income" | "landHolding" | "state"  │
     │   | "rationCard" | "occupation" | "housing" | "gender" | "other";       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ export interface Criterion {                                            │
     │   id: string;                                                           │
     │   labelEn: string;                                                      │
     │   labelHi: string;                                                      │
     │   type: CriterionType;                                                  │
     │   /** Pure. Returns null when the profile does not carry enough to      │
     │ decide. */                                                              │
     │   test: (p: CitizenProfile) => boolean | null;                          │
     │   ruleTextEn: string;   // the rule as the official source states it    │
     │   ruleTextHi: string;                                                   │
     │ }                                                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ export interface Scheme {                                               │
     │   id: string;                                                           │
     │   nameEn: string;                                                       │
     │   nameHi: string;                                                       │
     │   ministry: string;                                                     │
     │   jurisdiction: "union" | "state";                                      │
     │   criteria: Criterion[];                                                │
     │   benefitEn: string;                                                    │
     │   benefitHi: string;                                                    │
     │   applyUrl: string;                                                     │
     │   sourceUrl: string;     // the official page you actually read         │
     │   verifiedOn: string;    // ISO date you read it                        │
     │   confidence: "verified" | "conflicting_sources" | "unverified";        │
     │   note?: string;         // what disagreed, if anything                 │
     │ }                                                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ If two official pages disagree, mark it conflicting_sources, write what │
     │ disagreed in note,                                                      │
     │ and show that in the interface. state-fees.ts already behaves this way  │
     │ for the Maharashtra                                                     │
     │ fee. It reads as rigour, not weakness.                                  │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The evaluator decides. No model touches it.                             │
     │ export type CriterionOutcome = "met" | "not_met" | "unknown";           │
     │                                                                         │
     │ export interface SchemeResult {                                         │
     │   schemeId: string;                                                     │
     │   verdict: "eligible" | "not_eligible" | "more_information_needed";     │
     │   criteria: { id: string; outcome: CriterionOutcome; ruleText: string   │
     │ }[];                                                                    │
     │ }                                                                       │
     │ eligible only when every criterion is met. not_eligible when any is     │
     │ not_met.                                                                │
     │ more_information_needed otherwise. Never guess an unknown into a met.   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The interface. Ask the smallest profile that discriminates across all   │
     │ eight, roughly six to                                                   │
     │ eight questions: age, state, annual household income band, land         │
     │ holding, ration card type,                                              │
     │ occupation, whether a pucca house is owned, and the gender of the child │
     │ for Sukanya. Then show                                                  │
     │ all eight, eligible first, each spelling out which criteria passed and  │
     │ which failed with the                                                   │
     │ rule text shown. Do not show a bare yes or no. Showing the working is   │
     │ the point: a citizen                                                    │
     │ who reads "you fail on land holding, the rule is X" learns something a  │
     │ yes or no never teaches.                                                │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Name myscheme.gov.in on the page and say what is different here.        │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The handoff. On not_eligible or more_information_needed: "Ask them why, │
     │ in writing".                                                            │
     │ Seed /start with a question asking the department for a certified copy  │
     │ of the criteria applied                                                 │
     │ and the recorded reason for rejection. That routes into the             │
     │ jurisdiction engine, which will                                         │
     │ correctly work out that most of these schemes are delivered by State    │
     │ departments even when the                                               │
     │ scheme itself is Central. That is a genuinely interesting thing for the │
     │ product to know and it                                                  │
     │ falls out of code that already exists.                                  │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Test, evaluate.test.ts: for each of the eight schemes, one profile that │
     │ is eligible, one                                                        │
     │ that fails on a named criterion, and one that is missing a field and    │
     │ must come back                                                          │
     │ more_information_needed. Plus one asserting every scheme has a          │
     │ non-empty sourceUrl and a                                               │
     │ verifiedOn date, so an unverified scheme cannot ship by accident.       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Commit: Add the scheme eligibility reader, decided in code and shown    │
     │ working                                                                 │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ WP7: Hindi and voice                                                    │
     │                                                                         │
     │ New files:                                                              │
     │ src/lib/i18n/en.ts                                                      │
     │ src/lib/i18n/hi.ts                                                      │
     │ src/lib/i18n/index.ts                                                   │
     │ src/components/LanguageToggle.tsx                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ A flat key to string dictionary and a t(key) function. No i18n library. │
     │ The surface is a                                                        │
     │ few hundred strings and every library in this space brings a routing    │
     │ model this app does not                                                 │
     │ need.                                                                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ // src/lib/i18n/index.ts                                                │
     │ export type Lang = "en" | "hi";                                         │
     │ export const LANG_COOKIE = "adhikaar_lang";                             │
     │                                                                         │
     │ export function getLang(): Promise<Lang>;          // server, reads the │
     │ cookie                                                                  │
     │ export function t(lang: Lang, key: keyof typeof en): string;            │
     │ Keys are dotted and grouped: nav.myFilings, interview.problem.title,    │
     │ deadline.daysLeft, rights.noSource, schemes.notEligible. Both           │
     │ dictionaries export the same                                            │
     │ shape, and hi.ts is typed as Record<keyof typeof en, string> so a       │
     │ missing key is a compile                                                │
     │ error rather than a blank on screen.                                    │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Language lives in a cookie so it survives a reload and is readable in   │
     │ Server Components with no                                               │
     │ client round trip. The toggle sits in SiteHeader.tsx and is visible on  │
     │ every page.                                                             │
     │                                                                         │
     │ When the interface is Hindi, apply var(--font-devanagari), already      │
     │ wired to                                                                │
     │ Tiro_Devanagari_Hindi in layout.tsx. Latin numerals throughout,         │
     │ including in Hindi,                                                     │
     │ because that is what Indian government forms and portals use.           │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Translation honesty. Translate the interface by hand. Do not machine    │
     │ translate the UI.                                                       │
     │ Statutory text stays in English with a Hindi explanation beside it,     │
     │ never a Hindi replacement.                                              │
     │ Section 6(1) permits an application in English, Hindi or the local      │
     │ official language, and the                                              │
     │ generated PDF must stay in the language it will actually be filed in. A │
     │ Hindi translation of a                                                  │
     │ statutory quotation shown as if it were the statute is a real defect,   │
     │ not a nicety.                                                           │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Voice. VoiceDictationButton.tsx already has an EN and HI toggle built   │
     │ in. Do not add                                                          │
     │ a second one. Give it an optional defaultLang prop and pass the app     │
     │ language so it starts on                                                │
     │ the right one. Use it on the interview's problem step, which it already │
     │ effectively is through                                                  │
     │ GrievanceField, and add it to the Rights Navigator question box. It     │
     │ already returns null when                                               │
     │ the browser has no SpeechRecognition, so there is no broken-control     │
     │ case to handle.                                                         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Check: toggle to Hindi and walk the whole interview, the Rights         │
     │ Navigator and the scheme                                                │
     │ checker without hitting an untranslated string. Then set the OS to      │
     │ reduced motion and confirm                                              │
     │ nothing breaks, since the loader and citation popover both branch on    │
     │ it.                                                                     │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Commit: Make the whole interface bilingual, with dictation in both      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ WP8: The clock that actually runs                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ The sweep currently runs when somebody clicks a button labelled "demo   │
     │ control". A judge will                                                  │
     │ read that as fake, and they will be right.                              │
     │                                                                         │
     │ New files: src/app/api/cron/sweep/route.ts, vercel.json                 │
     │ Edited: src/app/my/[id]/page.tsx, src/app/my/page.tsx                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ 1. The route calls listAllOpenCases() from WP1, runs sweepCase(record,  │
     │ new Date()) on each,                                                    │
     │ and persists anything that changed. Pace the writes against the 30 per  │
     │ minute limiter                                                          │
     │ (Trap 7), the same way scripts/seed-cases.ts already does. Return a     │
     │ JSON summary of how                                                     │
     │ many were checked and how many changed.                                 │
     │ 2. Guard it: if (req.headers.get("authorization") !== \Bearer           │
     │ ${process.env.CRON_SECRET}`)return 401. Vercel sends this header        │
     │ automatically whenCRON_SECRET` is set in the project                    │
     │ environment.                                                            │
     │ 3. vercel.json:                                                         │
     │ { "crons": [{ "path": "/api/cron/sweep", "schedule": "0 3 * * *" }] }   │
     │ 3. Vercel's Hobby plan allows daily crons only, so do not write a more  │
     │ frequent schedule. Once                                                 │
     │ a day at 03:00 UTC is right for a thirty day clock.                     │
     │ 4. Keep the manual trigger, but reframe it. It stops being a demo       │
     │ control and becomes a real                                              │
     │ feature: "Show me what happens if they miss the deadline", which        │
     │ simulates a future date                                                 │
     │ and shows the citizen the first appeal that will be drafted on their    │
     │ behalf. That is a                                                       │
     │ genuinely reassuring thing to be able to see on day two, and it doubles │
     │ as the demo without                                                     │
     │ pretending to be something it is not.                                   │
     │ 5. On /my, a filing whose deadline has lapsed gets a distinct row       │
     │ treatment and its drafted                                               │
     │ first appeal is one click away.                                         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Check: curl the route with and without the bearer token. Confirm a      │
     │ seeded overdue case                                                     │
     │ transitions to first_appeal_drafted and its appeal text lands in        │
     │ operatorNotes citing                                                    │
     │ sections 7(2), 19(1) and 7(6).                                          │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Commit: Run the statutory sweep on a real daily cron                    │

     │   A tool that admits a gap is more convincing than one that never does. │
     │ - One line on scope: it drafts, it never submits.                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ ## Partner email                                                        │

     │ address used at                                                         │
     │ registration. There is no recovery path if it is wrong.                 │

     │ ---                                                                     │
     │ §8 If you get stuck                                                     │
     │                                                                         │
     │ - A test fails in src/lib that you did not touch. You probably changed  │
     │ a shared type.                                                          │
     │ Check CaseRecord. Revert and add the field as optional instead.         │
     │ - typecheck fails on a type you never wrote, like LayoutProps. Trap 1.  │
     │ Run                                                                     │
     │ npm run build first.                                                    │
     │ - Middleware throws about crypto. Trap 2. Move the verification out of  │
     │ middleware.                                                             │
     │ - A Firebase admin call fails with a permission error. Trap 3. You are  │
     │ calling something                                                       │
     │ that needs more than roles/datastore.user. Find another way rather than │
     │ widening the role.                                                      │
     │ - Firestore asks you to create an index. Trap 4. Drop the orderBy and   │
     │ sort in JavaScript.                                                     │
     │ - A new element has rounded corners you did not ask for, or square ones │
     │ you did not want.                                                       │
     │ Trap 5. The global [class] { border-radius: 0 } rule.                   │
     │ - A Server Action runs but the page does not navigate. Trap 6.          │
     │ Something swallowed the                                                 │
     │ redirect().                                                             │
     │ - A bulk write dies partway through. Trap 7. Pace it against the rate   │
     │ limiter.                                                                │
     │ - You are about to let Gemini decide something. Stop. Rule 3 in §0.     │
     │ That is the line the                                                    │
     │ whole product is built on, and crossing it is the only way to lose this │
     │ on the merits.                                                          │
     ╰─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────╯

● Found a real bug via this test, not a test artifact: the session cookie is
  marked Secure whenever NODE_ENV === "production", but npm run start locally
  serves plain http://, so the browser silently drops the cookie. Vercel itself
  is always HTTPS so production would be fine, but this blocks all local QA
  against a production build. Fixing it properly — deriving secure from the
  actual request protocol instead of NODE_ENV.

  Read 1 file (ctrl+o to expand)

  The standard fix is a hard navigation after auth state changes rather than
  router.push. Fixing LoginForm.tsx.

● Real bugs, not test artifacts — the floor was normalizing by each query's own
  max score, which trivially inflates a lone weak match to 1.0, defeating the
  floor's purpose. Plus a missing singular/plural stem caused a ranking miss.
